## Best date plans

### 2026-10-03 — Third United Lenape / Lunáapeew Nations Pow Wow + Winner on Franklin

**Third United Lenape / Lunáapeew Nations Pow Wow, then a meal.** Bakery and cafe at Winner on Franklin. It is a 20-minute walk from the venue. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=171+East+Drive%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11225&destination=367+7th+Avenue%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11215&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.prospectpark.org/event/third-united-lenape-lunaapeew-nations-pow-wow/2026-10-03/) · [Restaurant](https://www.winner.nyc/)

## What to do this week

### 2026-10-01

#### [Mathematics and the Art of M.C. Escher — Opening Celebration](https://momath.org/escher/)

6:30 PM · museums · Free opening celebration; exhibition admission later $30 general, $20 seniors/students · National Museum of Mathematics · Flatiron · Manhattan · Score 77.0  
MoMath opens the first major exhibition devoted to the mathematics behind M.C. Escher's prints, drawings, woodblocks, symmetry, geometry, infinity, perspective, and impossible worlds.
**Timing:** Free opening celebration 6:30-9pm; event is full, wait-list line admitted from 7:30pm as space permits.
**Practical note:** Registration is encouraged but the opening is full; wait-list admission begins at 7:30pm if space permits.
**Why now:** The exhibition opens on October 1 and the free celebration is this evening.
**Hot booking alert:** Official page says the opening event is full and wait-list admission is space-permitting.
[Official](https://momath.org/escher/) · [Tickets / booking](https://momath.org/escher/) · [Discovered via The Skint](https://theskint.com/)

#### [Slumber Party Comedy Show](https://unionhallny.com)

7:30 PM · comedy · $10–$15 · Union Hall · Park Slope · Brooklyn · Score 74.0  
Clare Donaher and Shelby Latterman host a campy stand-up hour at Union Hall with bits, confessions, and games.
**Timing:** Evening stand-up show.
**Practical note:** Union Hall has stairs to some event spaces; confirm accessibility if needed.
**Why now:** One-night comedy booking on October 1.
**Book soon:** Small-room comedy can sell through, and advance tickets are cheaper.

4:00 PM · museums · Free · Museum of Modern Art · Midtown · Manhattan · Score 47.6  
MoMA offers free admission on Friday evenings. Free tickets are limited, released in advance, and go quickly.
**Timing:** Free Friday evenings, roughly 4-8pm. Confirm the current window and ticket release on moma.org.
**Practical note:** Book the free timed ticket the moment it is released; the line for walk-ups is long.
**Why now:** A standing weekly free window at a museum that is otherwise one of the most expensive in the city.

#### [Third United Lenape / Lunáapeew Nations Pow Wow](https://www.prospectpark.org/event/third-united-lenape-lunaapeew-nations-pow-wow/2026-10-03/)

10:00 AM · festival · Free RSVP · LeFrak Center at Lakeside, Prospect Park · Prospect Park · Brooklyn · Score 79.9  
Lenape/Lunáapeew and allied Indigenous artists, creators, dancers, drummers, and artisan craft and food vendors gather for a free two-day public Pow Wow.
**Timing:** Vendors open 10am; Grand Entry at 11am.
**Practical note:** Mostly sunny and cooler; the event is outdoors, so bring layers for the evening.
**Why now:** This is the first day of a two-day annual Pow Wow in Prospect Park.
**On-sale watch:** RSVP is requested on the official page.

#### [Green-Wood After Hours](https://www.green-wood.com/event/green-wood-after-hours-49/)

6:00 PM · unusual · $30; $24 members · Green-Wood Cemetery · Greenwood Heights · Brooklyn · Score 71.0  
An after-dark cemetery walk through Green-Wood's history, legends, and secrets, including entry into the usually locked Catacombs.
**Timing:** After-dark walking tour; masks and costumes are not permitted.
**Practical note:** Tour is outdoors on hilly, uneven ground after dark; bring a flashlight and wear sturdy shoes.
**Why now:** Seasonal after-hours cemetery tours are running as October begins.
**Hot booking alert:** Small after-hours Green-Wood tours often sell out.

A guided tour on Brooklyn's oldest forest, covering historic stewardship, old trees, restoration work, and climate and pest challenges.
**Timing:** Guided walking tour with Prospect Park Alliance and Turnstile Tours.
**Practical note:** Mostly sunny with highs near 71; wear walking shoes for park trails.
**Why now:** The walk is part of citywide City of Forest Day programming on October 3.

## Where to eat

Verified against captured official menu/event evidence and Eater reporting for current prices, dates, openings, and duplicate offers as of Oct. 1, 2026.

Dining research: 2026-10-01T10:36:15+00:00

No candidate required a live Michelin Guide verification for a current NYC star. Eater, not Michelin directly, says Mexico City’s El Califa de León was the world’s first Michelin-starred taqueria in 2024; treat that as a historical Eater-sourced claim only, not a verified current NYC Michelin-star award.

### La Grande Boucherie $22 boeuf bourguignon lunch special

Deals & happy hours — La Grande Boucherie NYC
